Method: CheMatE基于ModernBERT骨干，采用两阶段训练：(1) 在从FineWeb和ChemPile构建的大规模SMILES标注长上下文科学文档语料（104亿和115亿token）上进行持续掩码语言建模；(2) 使用算法生成的SMILES-文本对合成数据集，通过Multiple Negative Ranking Loss进行Matryoshka对比学习。

Result: 在分子性质预测和科学语言理解等多个下游任务上，CheMatE与专用化学模型和通用语言模型基线相比均取得竞争性性能，验证了自建数据集与顺序训练策略相结合能产生鲁棒且高迁移性的表征。

Conclusion: 通过在单一文本框架内有效统一分子结构和上下文语义信号，CheMatE展示了将结构信息与语言语义联合建模的可行性，为化学领域的双语义表示学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Transformer models have revolutionized natural language processing (NLP), and text-based molecular representations like SMILES have successfully extended these architectures to chemistry. However, domain-adaptive pre-training often causes models to overfit to chemical syntax, catastrophically forgetting their foundational semantic capabilities. To address this challenge, we introduce CheMatE, a chemistry-oriented embedding model that jointly captures molecular structure and domain-specific natural language within the same representation space. Built on a ModernBERT backbone, CheMatE learns bi-semantic representations through a two-stage training procedure: continued masked language modeling (MLM) followed by a Matryoshka contrastive learning stage via Multiple Negative Ranking Loss (MNRL). First, we train the model using MLM on a novel, large-scale corpus of SMILES-annotated, long-context scientific documents that were constructed and curated from FineWeb and ChemPile (comprising 10.4B and 11.5B tokens, respectively). Subsequently, the model undergoes contrastive learning using a synthetic dataset of SMILES-text pairs algorithmically derived from our original training corpus. This design exposes the model to SMILES-enriched scientific literature, enabling bi-semantic understanding. We evaluate CheMatE across a range of downstream tasks covering molecular property prediction and scientific language understanding. Our results demonstrate that coupling our custom-curated datasets with this sequential training strategy yields robust, highly transferable representations. By effectively unifying structural and contextual signals within a single text-based framework, CheMatE achieves competitive performance across both specialized chemistry models and general-purpose language model baselines.

</details>


### [91] [CRS-Triage: Confidence- and Reliability-Aware Selective Triage under Incomplete Clinical Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03862)
*Guan Qiang,Yushen Chen,Tianlong Liu,David Rotenberg,Ethan H. Kim,Fang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRS-Triage，一种置信度与可靠性感知的选择性分诊模型，用于在急诊电子病历数据不完整、不可靠、不一致的情况下预测患者病情等级。模型通过分别评估结构化数据和临床文本的可靠性，并联合考虑两种模态间的一致性来估计预测置信度，同时通过惩罚欠分诊错误来偏好轻微过度分诊。在MIMIC-IV-ED数据集上的实验表明其预测性能强，风险-覆盖率权衡更优，且在数据退化时保持可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊分诊需要在极短时间内做出可靠决策，但可用的EHR数据（包括结构化数据和临床文本）往往不完整、不可靠且不一致。现有机器学习模型通常依赖完整可靠的数据来准确预测患者病情等级，这在现实急诊场景中难以满足，因此亟需一种能在数据质量不佳时仍保持可靠性的分诊预测方法。

Method: CRS-Triage采用选择性预测框架：分别评估结构化数据和临床文本两种模态的可靠性，然后联合考虑模态间一致性来估计每次预测的置信度分数。通过将置信度与预定义阈值比较，模型可选择性决定是否自主决策或将案例延迟交由人工处理。此外，通过在损失函数中惩罚欠分诊（漏诊高危患者）错误，模型偏向于给出略高的病情等级（过度分诊）以降低临床风险。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV-ED数据集上的实验表明，CRS-Triage取得了较强的预测性能，提供了更好的风险-覆盖率权衡（risk-coverage trade-off），并且在EHR数据不完整、退化或模态间不一致的情况下仍能保持可靠的分诊能力。

Conclusion: CRS-Triage有效解决了真实急诊环境中EHR数据质量不佳导致的分诊预测困难，通过置信度感知的选择性预测机制和过度分诊偏好策略，在保证预测能力的同时提升了模型的临床可靠性和安全性，为急诊分诊决策支持提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Emergency triage requires reliable decisions within a short time period. However, the available electronic health record (EHR) data, including structured data and clinical text, are often incomplete, unreliable, and inconsistent. This makes machine learning (ML)-based triage prediction more challenging, as existing ML models typically rely on complete and reliable EHR data to accurately predict patients' acuity levels. To address this, we propose confidence- and reliability-aware selective triage (CRS-Triage) to predict patients' acuity levels with a confidence score. By comparing the confidence score with a predefined threshold, CRS-Triage can selectively determine whether the model should make the decision or defer the case. Specifically, CRS-Triage separately evaluates the reliability of structured data and clinical text and then jointly considers the consistency between the two modalities to estimate the confidence of each prediction. Moreover, to reduce the risk of missing high-acuity patients, namely under-triage, CRS-Triage prefers to assign patients slightly higher acuity levels, namely over-triage, by penalizing under-triage errors. Experiments on the MIMIC-IV-ED dataset show that CRS-Triage achieves strong predictive performance. It also provides a better risk-coverage trade-off and remains reliable when the available EHR data are incomplete, degraded, or inconsistent across modalities.

</details>


### [92] [GENESIS: Towards Explainable Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03868)
*Abhinav Thorat,Ravi Kumar Kolla,Vishak K Bhat,Harsh Vardhan Singh Chauhan,Niranjan Pedanekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GENESIS是一个可解释的混合因果发现框架，通过将图构建分解为可审计的决策点（三节点结构基序识别、统计证据整合、领域知识补充），实现100%的决策可追溯性，并在SHD指标上优于纯统计方法、与SOTA LLM辅助方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM辅助混合因果发现方法虽然通过语义推理改善了结构恢复，但其推理过程对单个边的决策影响不透明，无法解释为何某条边被包含或排除在DAG中。在真实应用中不存在ground-truth DAG，每个结构决策都必须独立可证明，因此需要提出决策可追溯性这一正式要求。

Method: GENESIS框架将图构建分解为可解释的决策点。首先识别并评分三节点结构基序（链、叉、对撞子）以建立透明的结构先验，然后通过整合这些先验与观测证据逐步细化图，仅在统计证据不足时调用领域知识。每个边的决策都通过可审计的证据来源（统计证据、马尔可夫毯一致性、显式领域推理）来解析。

Result: 实验表明GENESIS在所有设置下实现100%的决策可追溯性；尽管增加了可解释性约束，仍在大多数基准数据集上、所有样本规模下，以结构汉明距离（SHD）指标持续优于纯统计CD方法，并达到与SOTA LLM辅助方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: GENESIS证明了可解释性可以成为因果发现的一等目标，且不会以牺牲性能为代价；通过可审计的证据来源保证每个边决策的可追溯性，为真实世界中没有ground-truth的因果推断场景提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Causal Discovery (CD) from observational data faces two fundamental challenges. First, purely statistical methods often lack the power to resolve structural ambiguities in low-sample regimes. Second, although LLM-assisted hybrid approaches improve structure recovery through semantic reasoning, the influence of that reasoning on individual edge decisions remains largely opaque. Consequently, existing hybrid methods fail to satisfy a fundamental requirement: explaining why a particular edge is included or excluded in the learned directed acyclic graph (DAG). This is critical in real-world applications, where no ground-truth DAG exists and every structural decision must be independently justified. We formalize this requirement as decision traceability, requiring every inferred edge to be supported by auditable statistical evidence, Markov Blanket consistency, or explicit domain reasoning. We propose GENESIS, an explainable hybrid CD framework that decomposes graph construction into interpretable decision points. GENESIS first identifies and scores three-node structural motifs, including chains, forks, and colliders, to establish transparent structural priors, then progressively refines the graph by integrating these priors with observational evidence, invoking domain knowledge only when statistical evidence is insufficient. By design, every edge decision is resolved through an auditable source of evidence. Experiments show that GENESIS achieves 100% decision traceability across all settings, establishing explainability as a first-class objective in causal discovery. Despite this additional requirement, GENESIS consistently outperforms purely statistical CD methods on the majority of benchmark datasets across all sample regimes in terms of Structural Hamming Distance (SHD), while achieving performance comparable to state-of-the-art LLM-assisted approaches.

</details>


### [93] [Enhancing VLM Reward Models Through Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03875)
*Pyrros Koussios,Chenhao Li,Xin Chen,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构感知微调(SAFT)，一种自监督方法，通过LoRA适配器利用任务固有的结构先验在线精炼VLM产生的噪声奖励信号，无需人工标注即提高RL策略收敛速度和对齐质量(EPIC距离)，表明奖励失败常源于结构脆弱性而非语义理解错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计奖励函数是RL的主要瓶颈，现有工作使用VLM作为奖励模型以文本-观测相似度替代人工设计，但这些奖励信号通常噪声大、不可靠，限制了其在部署中的直接效用。

Method: 提出Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT)：一种自监督方法，通过LoRA适配器在VLM的潜空间中引入内在结构先验作为正则化项，在线精炼不完美的奖励信号，无需ground-truth监督。评估覆盖不同基础模型能力的广泛谱系。

Result: SAFT持续地去除奖励景观中的噪声，使策略收敛更快，并显著改善与基础模型相比的对齐质量(EPIC距离)。结果表明许多失败可归因于结构脆弱性而非语义误解。

Conclusion: 通过用任务固有的结构归纳偏置替代大规模人工偏好标注，SAFT为稳定文本条件RL提供了可扩展路径，并强调了将任务结构作为通用归纳偏置的广泛价值。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions remains a major bottleneck in Reinforcement Learning (RL). Recent work uses large foundation Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as reward models, computing text-observation similarity to bypass manual reward engineering. Although promising, these rewards are often noisy and unreliable, limiting their direct utility during deployment. We present Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT), a simple, self-supervised method that refines these imperfect reward signals online without access to ground-truth supervision. SAFT leverages intrinsic structural priors to regularize the VLM's latent space via LoRA adapters. We rigorously evaluate SAFT across a spectrum of base model capabilities to demonstrate its versatility. Our results show that SAFT consistently denoises the reward landscape, yielding faster policy convergence and substantially improved alignment (EPIC distance) relative to the underlying base model, suggesting that failures can often be attributed to structural brittleness rather than semantic misunderstanding. By replacing extensive human preference annotation with structural inductive biases inherent to the task, SAFT offers a scalable path for stabilizing text-conditioned RL and underscores the broader value of incorporating task structure as a general inductive bias.

</details>


### [94] [Operationally Feasible Synthetic Power-Grid Scenarios via Learning the AC-Operable Joint Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03878)
*Chenhan Xiao,Xinyu He,Haoran Li,Hanghang Tong,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可行性感知的分布学习框架，通过分层扩散模型学习电力网拓扑、分支电气参数和时变负荷配置的可AC运行的联合分布，使得生成器本身直接产生运行可行的电网场景，无需事后优化处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成电网生成方法虽然通过事后验证、优化或物理感知生成提高了结构真实性和运行可行性，但生成的场景仍可能AC可行性和鲁棒性较低，限制了其在下游电力系统研究中的实用价值。

Method: 提出一个可行性感知的分布学习框架，将AC潮流收敛性和运行约束融入基于分层扩散的分布学习中。分层结构将高维生成任务分解为三个阶段：拓扑和母线属性生成、基于生成结构的分支参数生成、以及基于网络结构和电气特性的负荷配置生成。

Result: 在基准系统上的实验表明，该框架显著提高了运行可行性和裕度鲁棒性，同时保持了较强的统计保真度，并消除了基于优化的后处理。

Conclusion: 通过将可行性约束嵌入到生成过程本身而非事后强制，可以在保证统计真实性的同时实现运行可行的电网场景生成，为下游电力系统应用提供了更实用的合成场景。

Abstract: Synthetic power-grid scenarios are essential for planning, resilience assessment, contingency analysis, and data-driven power-system applications. Recent synthetic grid generation methods have improved structural realism and operational feasibility by incorporating engineering knowledge through post-generation validation, optimization, or physics-aware generation. However, generated scenarios may still exhibit low AC feasibility and robustness, limiting their practical value for downstream power-system studies. This paper proposes a feasibility-aware distribution-learning framework that learns the AC-operable joint distribution of network topology, branch electrical parameters, and time-varying load profiles. Instead of enforcing feasibility after generation, the proposed framework incorporates AC power-flow convergence and operational constraints into hierarchical diffusion-based distribution learning. This enables the generator itself to produce operationally feasible grid scenarios through efficient diffusion sampling. The hierarchical architecture decomposes the high-dimensional generation task into three engineering-motivated stages: topology and bus-attribute generation, branch-parameter generation conditioned on the generated structure, and load-profile generation conditioned on both network structure and electrical characteristics. Experiments on benchmark systems demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly improves operational feasibility and contingency robustness while maintaining strong statistical fidelity and eliminating optimization-based post-processing.

</details>


### [95] [Omega-S: A Functional Resilience Index for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03887)
*Alberto Acedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Omega-S是一种仅从权重矩阵计算的正则化惩罚项，用于缓解大语言模型微调时的灾难性遗忘。它无需旧数据、Fisher矩阵或旧权重副本，仅需三行代码即可集成到现有训练循环中，且计算开销增加不到4%。在Llama-3-8B上从代码微调到散文的任务中，Omega-S在HumanEval基准上保留原始能力的效果优于无正则化（保留率从62.9%提升到84.1%）、超参调优的权重衰减和EWC。论文还通过测量揭示了其真实机制是节点度方差的惩罚，而非其拓扑构造名称所暗示的内容，并首次量化了低秩语言模型微调中评估的方差（标准差0.104）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在微调新数据时会降低先前学到的能力（灾难性遗忘）。现有的缓解方法需要访问旧任务数据、Fisher矩阵或存储旧权重，这些要求在实际应用中往往不可满足。作者希望设计一种只用当前权重矩阵就能计算的正则化项，使其可以无缝、低成本地插入现有训练流程。

Method: Omega-S从权重矩阵本身计算一个惩罚项，形式上由Tr(A^3)构建（拓扑性质），但作者通过弹性分析测量了其四个组成因子中只有度方差项实际起作用（弹性约9e-3，其余三项弹性≤1e-4）。因此复合惩罚实际简化为对节点度方差的惩罚，在方阵模块中对应行幅值，在非方阵模块中对应方向对齐。论文同时报告了一个对比保持构造（contrast-preserving）虽然按设计工作但在全部十个种子上使保留率恶化。评估中作者还量化了相同配置重复运行的方差（保留率标准差0.104）。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B + LoRA从代码微调到散文的任务中：Omega-S在10个种子中的9个上保留能力优于无正则化（pass@1从0.173提升到0.238，保留率从62.9%提升到84.1%，符号检验p=0.011，Wilcoxon p=0.006）；在全部10个种子上优于调优的权重衰减（p=0.002）；在8/10个种子上优于调优的EWC（p=0.014）。所有对比在同一会话中重测。计算开销增加不到4%。此外，相同配置重复实验的保留率标准差为0.104，这为该领域的种子配对比较设定了显著性边界。

Conclusion: Omega-S提供了一种轻量、无需额外数据的正则化方法，可显著缓解微调中的灾难性遗忘。论文强调机制应被测量而非仅靠声称：其名义上的拓扑构造在实际梯度中退化为度方差惩罚，这一透明性报告对研究社区有重要价值。同时，论文首次量化了低秩语言模型微调评估的种子间方差，指出该方差是此领域所有配对比较的显著性基础。代码、逐种子结果及全部负面实验结果均已开源。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a large language model on new data degrades what it previously learned. We present Omega-S, a drop-in penalty computed from the weight matrix alone: it needs no previous-task data, no Fisher matrix and no stored copy of the old weights. It is three lines in an existing training loop and adds under 4% to the cost of a step.
  Retention. On Llama-3-8B with LoRA, fine-tuned from code to prose and measured by HumanEval over ten seeds, Omega-S retains more of the original capability than no regularisation on 9 of 10 seeds (0.173 -> 0.238 absolute pass@1; sign test one-sided p=0.011, Wilcoxon p=0.006), as a retention ratio, 62.9% -> 84.1%. It also beats tuned weight decay on 10 of 10 seeds (p=0.002) and tuned EWC on 8 of 10 (p=0.014), every arm re-measured in the same session.
  Mechanism, measured rather than asserted. Omega-S is topological by construction, its objective built from Tr(A^3), but we measured which of its four factors actually moves and three do not: their elasticity with respect to the weights is at or below 1e-4, against 9e-3 for the degree-variance term. As implemented, the composite reduces to a penalty on the variance of node degrees, which means row magnitude in square modules and directional alignment in non-square ones. We report this because a method whose name promises one thing and whose gradient does another should say so. We also enumerate the open design choices, including a contrast-preserving construction that does what it was designed to do and makes retention worse on all ten seeds.
  Repeating an identical configuration, same seed and same hardware, gives a standard deviation of 0.104 in retention ratio. We have not found this quantified for low-rank fine-tuning of language models, and it bounds every seed-paired comparison in this literature, ours included.
  Code, per-seed results and the full record of negative results are available.

</details>


### [96] [Cross-Model KV Cache Transfer in LLM Families: A Closed-Form Linear Mapping for Prefill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03893)
*Taekyung Heo,Rasoul Shafipour,Ritchie Zhao,Maximilian Golub,Mohammad Mahdi Kamani,Ritika Borkar,Makesh Tarun Chandran,Pantea Zardoshti,Bita Darvish Rouhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模型KV缓存迁移方法，让接收模型复用源模型的KV缓存以跳过prefill，基于跨模型KV间的线性结构设计闭式岭回归映射器，在六个模型对上保留73-98%的独立prefill准确率，运行速度比重新prefill快2.7-25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产部署中经常在不同规格的模型间切换（成本-质量级联、对话中途切换、路由），每次切换接收模型都需要从头进行prefill，造成大量计算开销。现有KV缓存只能在同一模型内复用，跨模型场景下无法直接转移，因此需要一种让接收模型重用源模型KV缓存、跳过prefill的方法。

Method: 设计了一个按头操作的闭式岭回归映射器，分三步：1) 对每个目标层从源模型中选择top-k最具预测力的层，拼接其KV作为输入；2) 在映射前从keys中移除RoPE，使拟合与位置无关且可跨上下文长度复用；3) 在500条FineWeb-Edu序列（每条1,024 token）的小校准集上拟合岭回归。对失败的两个模型对，进一步采用非线性MLP来恢复准确率。

Result: 在三个模型家族中六对模型上进行评估：四个模型对的线性映射器保留了接收模型独立prefill准确率的73-98%；两个模型对出现明显退化；MLP可在这两个失败对上恢复高达+37 pp的HellaSwag保持率。映射器运行速度比重新prefill快2.7-25倍，且在多轮对话交接中保持稳定。在Qwen3 14B→32B上，单层源模型可解释目标模型keys 56%和values 32%的方差，多层时分别达到79%和65%。

Conclusion: 跨模型KV缓存迁移在实践中是可行的，线性映射器在大多数模型对上高效且有效，非线性MLP可处理退化情形。这使得跨模型切换场景下可以跳过prefill，显著减少推理延迟和计算开销。

Abstract: Production deployments often swap between different-sized models in a family for cost-quality cascading, mid-conversation switching, and routing, and each swap forces the receiver to repay the prefill from scratch. We propose cross-model KV cache transfer, where the receiver reuses the source's KV cache, skipping prefill. We find that cross-model KV has substantial linear structure across matched-KV pairs, where source and target share KV head count and per-head dimension. On Qwen3 14B->32B, one source layer explains 56% of variance in the target's keys and 32% in values, rising to 79% and 65% with multiple source layers. Building on this, we design a closed-form ridge mapper that operates per head and proceeds in three steps. First, for each target layer we select the top-k most predictive source layers and concatenate their KV as input. Second, we strip RoPE from the keys before mapping, so the fit is position-free and reusable across context lengths. Third, we fit ridge regression on a small calibration set of 500 FineWeb-Edu sequences of 1,024 tokens each. Surprisingly, across six pairs in three families, this linear mapper retains 73-98% of the receiver's standalone-prefill accuracy on four pairs, while two degrade sharply. A nonlinear MLP recovers up to +37 pp HellaSwag retention on the failures. The mapper runs 2.7-25x faster than re-prefill and remains stable across multi-turn handoff, making cross-model KV cache transfer practical.

</details>


### [97] [Sparse Weight Decomposition for Efficient Circuit Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03913)
*Chuanhao Yan,Xuhan Huang,Yawen Duan,Zhenfei Yin,Hang Zhao,Bryan Dai,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏权重分解（SWD）方法，通过将预训练模型的每个权重矩阵分解为两个稀疏因子，利用共享的中间坐标作为可寻址的电路单元，无需训练替代网络即可提取电路。SWD在更少数据下匹敌甚至超越现有基线，并支持全模型替换和零数据变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路提取方法依赖学习辅助稀疏表示或训练稀疏模型，这需要大量额外计算，并在分析表示与原始预训练模型之间引入忠实性（fidelity）差距。作者希望在不牺牲解释忠实性的前提下，直接从预训练密集Transformer中获得可解释的电路单元。

Method: SWD将预训练的线性投影权重矩阵重新参数化，分解为两个稀疏因子的乘积，这两个因子的共享中间坐标作为可单独寻址的电路单元。该方法不需要训练独立的替代网络，而是直接在原始模型上支持与稀疏特征方法相同的评分、选择和消融电路提取流程。此外，SWD还提供零数据变体。

Result: 在单矩阵替换中，SWD以不到基线1%的数据取得了与Transcoder等强基线相当的保持忠诚度。在匹配替换忠实度下，SWD在GPT-2、Qwen2.5和Qwen3.5-27B上以更少的活跃读/写边和选中单元达到相同的电路充分性和必要性目标。SWD在微调非零因子值后对全模型（所有注意力与MLP权重矩阵）替换仍然有效。

Conclusion: SWD提供了一种高效、高忠实度的电路提取方案，显著降低数据需求并支持全模型规模的可解释性分析，甚至可在零数据场景下使用，为机制可解释性分析提供了更灵活的工具。

Abstract: Dense pretrained transformers do not naturally expose interpretable units for circuit extraction. Existing approaches obtain such units by learning auxiliary sparse representations or training sparse models, incurring substantial additional computation while potentially introducing a fidelity gap between the representation being analyzed and the original pretrained model. We propose Sparse Weight Decomposition (SWD), which reparameterizes pretrained linear projections by factorizing each weight matrix into two sparse factors whose shared intermediate coordinates serve as individually addressable circuit units. Without training a separate replacement network, this parametric representation supports the same scoring, selection, and ablation circuit extraction workflow used for methods that learn sparse features. Across single-matrix replacements, SWD matches the held-out fidelity achieved by Transcoder and other strong baselines while using less than 1% of the data that those baselines use to train their replacements. For matched replacement fidelity, SWD reaches the same circuit sufficiency and necessity targets with fewer active read/write edges and selected units across tasks on GPT-2, Qwen2.5, and Qwen3.5-27B. We further show that SWD remains effective for full-model replacement of all attention and MLP weight matrices after fine-tuning the nonzero factor values. Finally, SWD also features a zero-data variant, allowing broader use of mechanistic interpretability analysis (e.g., per-step analysis).

</details>


### [98] [Trajectory inference via Acceleration Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03916)
*Bartolo Dazzini,Giovanni Conforti,Alain Durmus,Aram-Alexandre Pooladian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Acceleration Matching (AM)的新算法，用于解决轨迹推断问题。该方法将插值问题提升到相空间，通过回归显式的条件加速度场来生成随机、平滑且符合给定边缘分布的轨迹，避免了预处理子程序和基于模拟的训练目标，在多个基准问题上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹推断是许多科学领域的基础问题，但现有算法存在计算瓶颈：要么依赖昂贵的预处理子程序来强制平滑性，要么依赖基于仿真的训练目标。这些方法在计算上开销较大，需要一种更高效的方法来克服这些局限。

Method: 提出Acceleration Matching (AM)算法。核心思想是将原始插值问题提升到相空间（即同时考虑位置和速度），然后回归到一个显式的条件加速度场，该加速度场能够诱导出随机且平滑的轨迹，并与给定的边缘分布（即离散时间点的观测快照）保持一致。训练过程仅需位置数据，无需轨迹模拟，也无需昂贵的预处理步骤。

Result: 通过大量数值实验证明，AM算法在现有文献中的多个基准问题上与现有算法相比具有竞争力或表现更优。

Conclusion: Acceleration Matching为轨迹推断提供了一种轻量级、高效的新方案，在计算效率和求解质量上均具有优势，有望成为该领域具有竞争力的工具。

Abstract: Trajectory inference is a fundamental problem in many scientific domains: given a collection of unpaired snapshots of observations at discrete time points, the goal is to generate smooth trajectories that best resemble and interpolate the data. Existing algorithms exhibit computational challenges: they either rely on preprocessing subroutines to enforce smoothness or on simulation-based training objectives, both of which can be expensive. In order to overcome these limitations, we propose a new algorithm called Acceleration Matching (\texttt{AM}). Our approach consists of lifting the original interpolation problem to phase space and then regressing onto an explicit conditional acceleration field that induces random, smooth trajectories that agree with the prescribed marginals. Importantly, our resulting training algorithm only requires positional data, avoids trajectory simulation during training, and is devoid of expensive preprocessing. We provide ample numerical evidence suggesting that \texttt{AM} is competitive with or superior to existing algorithms on several benchmark problems from the existing literature.

</details>


### [99] [PRISM: Powerful Time Series to Image (TS2I) Representations for Multivariate Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03926)
*Mateusz Smendowski,Kamil Faber,Piotr Nawrocki,Nathalie Japkowicz,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一个即插即用的元工作流，用于系统性地构建和评估基于图像表示的多变量时间序列异常检测方法。通过超过7000个实验，PRISM配置在14个数据集中的10个上达到最佳VUS-PR，平均提升41%。文章还发现多通道图像的通道化(channelization)是关键且此前未被充分研究的设计维度，并提出新的基于统计的MSM方案，取得11-27%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列异常检测(TSAD)在预测维护、金融和云计算等领域至关重要，但性能对表示选择高度敏感，尤其是在多变量场景下。尽管将时间序列转换为图像在预测和分类任务中已获成功，但多变量高维序列如何映射到多通道图像、以及视觉骨干网络能否匹配时间域基线在TSAD中的表现，这些问题仍不清楚。

Method: PRISM是一个即插即用的元工作流，允许系统性地构建和评估基于图像的多变量时间序列异常检测表示方法。作者对14个数据集进行了超过7000个实验，评估了多种PRISM配置与24个时间域基线的对比。此外，作者识别并深入研究了"通道化"这一关键设计维度，提出了基于统计学的MSM(Multi-channel Statistics-based Mapping)方案来替代PCA等方法。

Result: 在14个数据集中，精心设计的PRISM配置在10个数据集上取得了最佳VUS-PR，平均比最佳对比方法提升41%。MSM方案相对PCA替代方案获得了11-27%的性能提升。ImageNet预训练编码器可以有效地迁移到TSAD任务，冻结编码器保留了微调性能的92%，同时训练速度快1.8倍。

Conclusion: 基于图像的表示方法通过适当配置（尤其关注通道化设计）可以与甚至超越传统时间域基线在TSAD中的表现。冻结的ImageNet预训练编码器在保持高性能的同时大幅降低训练成本，为TSAD提供了高效且强大的新路径。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) underpins applications in predictive maintenance, finance, and cloud computing, however performance remains sensitive to representation choices, especially in multivariate settings. While transforming time series into images has shown success in forecasting and classification, it remains unclear how multivariate, high-dimensional series should be mapped to multi-channel images and whether vision backbones can match time-domain baselines in TSAD. We introduce PRISM, a plug-and-play meta-workflow enabling systematic construction and evaluation of image-based representations for multivariate TSAD. Our evaluation spanning over 7,000 experiments shows that well-designed PRISM configurations are competitive with 24 time-domain baselines, achieving the best VUS-PR on 10 of 14 datasets, with an average improvement of 41% over the best competing method on those datasets. Further, we identify channelization - how the channel dimension of multi-channel images is constructed - as a critical and previously understudied design dimension, and introduce MSM, a novel statistics-based scheme achieving 11-27% gains over PCA-based alternatives. Finally, ImageNet-pretrained encoders transfer effectively to TSAD, with frozen encoders retaining 92% of fine-tuned performance while training 1.8 times faster. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Smendowski/PRISM.

</details>


### [100] [A Physics-Flavored Transformer Network for Parametrizing Contraction Dynamics of Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03927)
*Mattias Luber,Timo Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理特征神经网络（PFNN），将拉伸指数物理模型集成到CNN-Transformer架构中，用于自动化骨骼肌组织（ESMs）的动力学表型分析。通过混合训练范式（合成数据预训练+真实数据无监督自对齐），该方法能够直接从力-时间曲线中提取物理有意义的参数，实现高保真的收缩表型参数化，并适用于杜氏肌营养不良等多种细胞系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程化骨骼肌组织的功能表征通常只依赖峰值力等简单指标，忽略了关键的动力学信息。这是因为机制模型虽然能捕捉这些动力学特征，但其数学复杂性阻碍了规模化应用和领域内的广泛采用。因此需要一种自动化、可扩展的方法来提取动力学表型信息。

Method: 提出PFNN架构，将拉伸指数物理模型嵌入CNN-Transformer中，实现从力-时间曲线直接提取物理参数。采用混合训练策略：先在合成数据上训练模型建立"物理直觉"，再在未标注的真实实验数据上进行无监督自对齐，以解决标注生物数据稀缺的问题。

Result: 该物理特征方法在多种收缩表型和细胞系（包括杜氏肌营养不良模型）上实现了高保真的参数化，展示了可扩展、自改进的管道，有效弥合了理想生物物理学与噪声体外数据之间的差距。

Conclusion: PFNN为高通量生物物理研究提供了一个强大的自动化工具，将物理模型的可解释性与深度学习的表达能力相结合，克服了传统机制模型复杂性和数据稀缺的双重挑战。

Abstract: Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues (ESMs) have become a key structure for biomedical disease modeling and pharmacological screening, yet their functional characterization often relies on simplistic metrics like peak force, discarding critical kinetic information. This is partially due to the high level of mathematical complexity which mechanistic models introduce to capture these dynamics. Hence, exactly the complexity prevents scalable application and widespread adaptation in the field. Here we present a Physics-Flavored Neural Network (PFNN) that automates the kinetic phenotyping of ESMs. Our architecture integrates a stretched-exponential physical model into a CNN-Transformer, enabling the extraction of physically meaningful parameters directly from force-time profiles. To address the scarcity of labeled biological data, we employ a hybrid training paradigm: the model develops a "physical intuition" on synthetic data before undergoing unsupervised self-alignment on unlabeled real-world measurements. Our results demonstrate that this physics-flavored approach achieves high-fidelity parameterization across diverse contractile phenotypes and cell lines, including Duchenne Muscular Dystrophy models. Our scalable, self-improving pipeline bridges the gap between idealized biophysics and noisy \emph{in vitro} data, providing a robust tool for high-throughput biophysical research.

</details>


### [101] [Latent Reward Registers for Diffusion Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03929)
*Yuanshen Guan,Zipeng Feng,Zhiwei Xiong,Peiqin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latent Reward Registers机制，通过在冻结的DiT输入序列中前置可学习的无位置寄存器token，直接从中间噪声隐变量估计终端偏好奖励，解决扩散模型多步去噪过程中的时间信用分配问题。该机制产生密集可微的奖励信号，支持两种对齐策略：训练端RG-OPD通过沿策略内轨迹蒸馏奖励引导更新，避免标准策略梯度的高成本rollout；推理端RGS通过幅度匹配的奖励梯度引导采样无需参数更新。实验表明寄存器在高噪声水平下取得最高配对准确率，RG-OPD性能优于在线RL基线且GPU耗时减少最多33倍，RGS在无训练方法中达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与人类偏好对齐通常依赖最终生成样本上的稀疏终端奖励，这在整个多步去噪过程中造成严重的时间信用分配问题，因为中间噪声隐变量无法获得有效的奖励信号反馈。

Method: 1) 提出Latent Reward Registers：在冻结的DiT输入序列前前置可学习、位置自由的寄存器token，从中间噪声隐变量直接估计终端偏好，不改变生成器隐藏状态或速度场。2) 训练策略RG-OPD：沿策略内轨迹蒸馏奖励引导的更新，避免标准策略梯度昂贵的rollout。3) 推理策略RGS：通过幅度匹配的奖励梯度引导采样轨迹，无需参数更新。

Result: 1) 在高噪声水平(u=0.8)下，寄存器在评估的潜在奖励模型中达到最高配对准确率。2) RG-OPD优于在线强化学习基线，同时GPU训练时间减少最多33倍。3) RGS在无训练方法中建立新SOTA，同时提升对齐指标和感知质量指标。

Conclusion: 通过寄存器token实现从中间噪声隐变量直接估计终端奖励，有效解决了稀疏奖励带来的时间信用分配问题，所提出的训练和推理两种对齐策略在效率和性能上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Aligning diffusion models with human preferences usually relies on a sparse terminal reward evaluated on the final generated samples, presenting a severe temporal credit-assignment challenge across the multi-step denoising process. We propose Latent Reward Registers, a mechanism that estimates terminal preference directly from intermediate noisy latents by prepending learnable, position-free register tokens to the input sequence of a frozen Diffusion Transformer (DiT). This independent readout mechanism extracts latent reward evidence without altering the generator's hidden states or velocity field. The resulting dense, differentiable reward signal throughout the full denoising process facilitates two alignment strategies. For training, Reward-Gradient On-Policy Distillation (RG-OPD) distills reward-guided updates along on-policy trajectories, bypassing the computationally expensive rollouts of standard policy gradients. For inference, Reward-Guided Sampling (RGS) steers trajectories via magnitude-matched reward gradients without parameter updates. Empirically, at high noise levels (u = 0.8), the registers reach the highest pairwise accuracy among the evaluated latent reward models. Furthermore, RG-OPD outperforms online reinforcement learning baselines while reducing GPU hours by up to 33x, and RGS establishes a new state-of-the-art among training-free methods, strictly enhancing both alignment and perceptual metrics. Code and weights are available at https://github.com/Guanys-dar/latent-reward-register

</details>


### [102] [Muon Meets Mamba: Spectral Optimization for State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03941)
*Arslan Battalov,Karim Kramin,Alexander Markotenko,Sofia Sinitsina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了Muon优化器与AdamW在Mamba-2 130M状态空间模型上的性能，发现Muon的收益是局部化的：仅对输出投影使用Muon优于对输入投影或两者同时使用，优势主要体现为token效率，且条件数无法解释该收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon是一种通过在谱范数下进行最陡下降（结合Newton-Schulz迭代正交化权重矩阵更新）的新型优化器，目前几乎所有证据都来自Transformer模型，其在状态空间模型（SSM）上的行为缺乏系统性报告。作者希望填补这一空白，探究Muon在Mamba-2等SSM架构上的适用性，并精确定位其收益来源。

Method: 在Mamba-2 130M模型上，采用受控实验协议，将Muon与AdamW进行比较。关键设计是仅改变使用Muon训练的具体权重组（输入投影、输出投影或两者），从而隔离Muon收益的来源。实验在两种语料和两种token预算下进行，并允许训练超过计算最优点以检验收益的持久性，同时测量各投影的条件数以检验条件数是否解释性能增益。

Result: Muon的收益是局部化的：仅在输出投影上使用Muon时性能最优，超过对输入投影使用或对两者都使用的方案。收益主要体现在token效率方面，并且在两种语料和两种token预算下均成立，即使训练远超计算最优点后依然持续。Muon确实降低了所训练投影的条件数，但条件数更好的输入投影反而不带来性能提升，因此条件数无法解释该增益。

Conclusion: Muon在SSM（Mamba-2）上的收益是局部且特异的，集中于输出投影而并非输入投影，且该优势无法用条件数或预处理理论来解释。这一发现提示Muon的收益机制可能与其他因素（如输出投影的梯度统计特性或与其他优化器的互补作用）有关，需要在更广泛的架构中重新审视Muon的优势来源。

Abstract: Muon is a recent optimizer that orthogonalizes the update to each weight matrix with a Newton-Schulz iteration, which performs steepest descent under the spectral norm. Almost all the evidence for it comes from Transformer models, and its behavior on state-space models is largely unreported. We compare Muon with AdamW on Mamba-2 130M under a controlled protocol that varies only which weight groups are trained with Muon. The benefit is localized. Muon on the output projection alone beats Muon on the input projection or on both. The advantage is mainly one of token efficiency. It holds on two corpora and two token budgets, and persists when training continues well past the compute-optimal point. Conditioning does not explain the gain. Muon lowers the condition number of whichever projection it trains, but the better-conditioned input projection is not the one that helps.

</details>


### [103] [Assessment of Conditional Diffusion Model for Synthetic Histopathology Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03990)
*Seyed Kahaki,Shijie Li,Weijie Chen,Nicholas Petrick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对合成组织病理学图像的质量评估问题，指出现有指标（如FID和IS）依赖ImageNet预训练特征提取器，在病理图像上存在局限性。研究者提出使用基于数字病理数据集预训练的基础模型来修正FID和IS指标，并补充精确率-召回率指标，通过对比下游核分割任务性能验证了病理特异性指标具有更好的判别力和相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成组织病理学图像生成有望解决计算病理学中的数据稀缺问题，但现有评估方法可能无法充分评估合成医学图像的质量。传统指标（FID、IS）基于ImageNet预训练特征提取器，可能不适用于病理图像领域。

Method: 使用条件去噪扩散模型在四个基准数据集上训练（采用两步训练方法），生成具有系统性变化的质量特征的合成数据集。提出使用数字病理数据集预训练的基础模型来修正FID和IS指标，并补充精确率-召回率指标作为额外质量评估。同时测量合成数据质量指标与下游核分割性能（AJI+和Dice系数）的相关性。

Result: 病理特异性指标具有更好的判别力。修正后的Inception Score与下游任务性能的相关性更高（r=0.6096, p=0.0122），而原始IS相关性很低（r=0.0708, p=0.7944）。此外，增加生成训练数据的多样性相比提升单张生成图像的视觉保真度，与分割模型性能的正相关性更高。

Conclusion: 病理特异性评估指标比通用指标更适合评估合成病理图像质量；生成数据的多样性对下游模型性能的贡献大于图像视觉保真度的优化。

Abstract: Synthetic histopathology image generation has emerged as an approach that may address data scarcity in computational pathology, yet current evaluation methodologies may not fully assess synthetic data quality for medical applications. This work investigates and addresses limitations in existing evaluation metrics, investigating an approach for assessing synthetic histopathology image quality through domain-specific metrics and downstream task validation. We show that conventional synthetic data evaluation metrics such as Frechet Inception Distance (FID) and Inception Score (IS) may have limitations when applied to histopathology images due to their reliance on ImageNet-pretrained feature extractors. To address these limitations, we propose for consideration modified FID and IS approaches utilizing foundation models pretrained on digital pathology datasets, supplemented by precision-recall based metrics as part of an additional quality assessment. Using conditional denoising diffusion models trained on four benchmark datasets, with a two-step training approach, we generated synthetic datasets with systematically varied quality characteristics. We also measured the correlation between the synthetic data quality metrics with downstream nuclei segmentation performance using common metrics including the aggregated Jaccard index (AJI+) and the Dice coefficient. The study results suggest that pathology-specific metrics may provide improved discriminative power. Specifically, the modified Inception Score indicates higher correlation with downstream task performance (r=0.6096 with AJI+, p=0.0122), compared to the original IS (r=0.0708, p=0.7944). Our observations indicate that increasing the variety of generated training data has a higher positive correlation with segmentation model performance than improving the visual fidelity of individual generated images.

</details>


### [104] [Test-Time Scaling in Reasoning LLMs: Inference Regimes, Evaluation, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04001)
*Mohsen Hariri,Weicong Chen,Nahal Shahini,Vikash Singh,Kai Ye,Amirhossein Samandar,Debargha Ganguly,Sreehari Sankar,Yanyan Zhang,Shouren Wang,Jerry Peng,Biyao Zhang,Michael Hinczewski,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地构建了测试时扩展（test-time scaling）的理论框架，将其形式化为自回归模型隐式前缀树上的预算推理，区分了三种结构性扩展范式（单轨迹顺序扩展、叶级扩展和前缀级扩展），并提出了评估原则、可复现性要求以及一个包含超过20亿条推理轨迹的大规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展涵盖了多种结构迥异的推理算法（单轨迹扩展、候选采样聚合、部分状态搜索），但这些算法在统计结构、计算账目和失败模式上差异显著。目前将这些不同过程混为一谈，仅用单一标量"预算"或仅报告准确率而不说明推理协议，导致跨研究结果难以比较。

Method: 作者从三个轴系统化测试时扩展：(1) 将测试时扩展形式化为预算推理，区分单轨迹顺序扩展、带终态归约的叶级扩展和前缀级扩展三种结构范式；(2) 将被评估对象视为整个推理系统，引入一个评估画像（evaluation profile），其坐标和简单函数可恢复或界定常见的重复采样指标，并要求协议匹配的计算与不确定性报告；(3) 规定推理协议的可复现性要求，区分精确重放与分布可复现性，并识别各自所需的工件。同时按模型端和接口机制组织开源权重推理生态系统，并发布超过20亿条推理轨迹。

Result: 作者在广泛知识、符号推理和竞赛数学等多个基准上应用了这些原则，并组装了超过20亿条完整推理轨迹的数据集，附带逐步丰富的验证器（verifier）和词元级（token-level）信号供发布使用。

Conclusion: 本文认为，测试时扩展的结果必须在推理协议的上下文中报告，不同扩展结构应被视为不同的研究对象而非可互换的"预算"用法。通过统一的评估画像和可复现性规范，可以显著提高该领域跨研究的可比性和科学严谨性。

Abstract: Large language models can solve substantially harder reasoning problems with more inference-time compute. The term "test-time scaling," however, now covers diverse inference algorithms that extend deliberation along a single trajectory, sample completed candidates and aggregate them through voting or verification, or search over unfinished partial states. These algorithms differ in their statistical structure, compute accounting, and failure modes. Treating these procedures as interchangeable under a single scalar "budget," or reporting accuracy without the inference protocol that produced it, makes results difficult to compare across studies. We develop a systematic account of test-time scaling along three axes. First, we formalize test-time scaling as budgeted inference over the implicit prefix tree of an autoregressive model and distinguish three structural regimes: single-trajectory sequential scaling, leaf-level scaling with terminal reduction, and prefix-level scaling. Second, we treat the evaluated object as the entire inference system and develop evaluation principles that separate end-to-end system performance from candidate-bank diagnostics. We introduce an evaluation profile whose coordinates and simple functionals recover or bound common repeated-sampling metrics, and prescribe protocol-matched reporting of compute and uncertainty. Third, we specify reproducibility requirements for inference protocols, distinguishing exact replay from distributional reproducibility and identifying the artifacts needed to support each. We also organize the open-weight reasoning ecosystem by model-side and interface mechanisms, apply these principles to broad-knowledge, symbolic-reasoning, and competition-mathematics benchmarks, and assemble over 2 billion full reasoning traces for release with progressively richer verifier and token-level signals.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Separating Intelligence from Inference: A Standard for Edge-Native AI Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02608)
*Venkat Vinjam,Krishnaiah Narukulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI基础设施（3000亿美元集中式数据中心）将模型训练与推理混淆在同一物理硬件上是重大架构低效，提出"分离原则"：训练集中进行并以软件形式分发，推理在靠近数据源的边缘硬件上执行。量化显示，为10亿日活用户部署全边缘推理架构每年可节省约19 TWh电力和7.3兆吨CO2排放。论文还提出了两种新设备类别（PAC个人AI计算机和CAW企业AI工作站）以及包含八个组件的参考架构栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于AI行业已投入3000亿美元建设集中式数据中心，但用于支撑的大语言模型推理工作负载在架构上并不要求集中化。作者指出当前架构的根本低效在于将训练（不可约的集中化、资本密集、每版本仅一次）与推理（可并行化、延迟敏感、每次查询重复发生）混在同一物理硬件上，这造成了巨大的能源浪费和碳排放。

Method: 论文提出"分离原则"（separation principle）：训练集中完成后以软件形式分发，推理在数据源附近的边缘硬件上执行。具体包括：量化在文明尺度上的能源影响；定义两种新设备类别（PAC和CAW），给出具体硬件层级、内存带宽要求、热设计功耗包络和软件接口；描述包含八个组件的参考架构栈，涵盖权重分发、主权感知路由、热自适应量化、多租户资源管理、联邦网络推理、密码学溯源、隐私保护遥测和分布式上下文窗口扩展。

Result: 分析表明，面向10亿日活用户的全边缘驻留推理架构相比当前集中式实践，每年可节省约19 TWh电力和7.3兆吨CO2排放。论文给出了PAC和CAW两类新设备的硬件规格定义，并提出了包含八个组件的完整参考架构栈。其中多个组件已由第一作者提交美国专利申请，作为候选的开放架构原则呈现。

Conclusion: 作者认为AI基础设施应当遵循训练与推理的架构分离原则，将推理从集中的数据中心迁移到边缘设备，这不仅能大幅降低能源消耗和碳排放，还能改善延迟敏感性。论文提供了一套从设备规格到软件架构的完整参考方案，以支撑这一范式转变。

Abstract: The artificial intelligence industry has constructed a USD 300 billion centralized data center infrastructure to serve a workload, large language model inference, that does not architecturally require centralization. This paper articulates the central architectural inefficiency of contemporary AI infrastructure: the conflation of model training (irreducibly centralized, capital-intensive, one-time per model version) with model inference (parallelizable, latency-sensitive, recurring per query) on the same physical hardware. We propose the separation principle: intelligence is trained centrally and shipped as software; inference executes on hardware near the data source, at the edge. We quantify the energy implications at civilizational scale and show that a fully edge-resident inference architecture for one billion daily users saves approximately 19 TWh per year and 7.3 megatons of CO2 annually relative to current centralized practice. We specify two new device classes, the Personal AI Computer (PAC) and the Corporate AI Workstation (CAW), with concrete hardware tiers, memory bandwidth requirements, thermal envelopes, and software interfaces. We then describe a reference architectural stack of eight components addressing weight distribution, sovereignty-aware routing, thermal-adaptive quantization, multi-tenant resource management, federated network inference, cryptographic provenance, privacy-preserving telemetry, and distributed context window extension. Several components are the subject of pending United States patent applications by the first author and are presented here as candidate open architectural principles

</details>


### [106] [KernelBrain: Coarse-to-Fine, Budget-Aware Search for Agentic GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02611)
*Shuai Che,Gang Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个实用的GPU内核优化智能体，结合LLM引导的变异、自适应资源分配、策略门控评估和性能分析诊断，在Triton内核生成任务上实现了0.88x-6.72x相对PyTorch的加速，较最先进内核智能体最高1.4x加速，优化时间降低48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化自动化在实践中仍然困难：生成的变体可能违反正确性约束、运行时测量存在噪声、搜索过程容易过早停滞。

Method: 提出一个实用优化智能体，采用四个关键组件：(1) LLM引导的变异生成候选内核；(2) 自适应资源分配，用低成本评估筛选大量候选，将高保真度预算仅分配给有潜力的存活者；(3) 策略门控评估以控制评估过程；(4) 性能分析器提供诊断信息指导优化方向。

Result: 在重要Triton内核生成任务上，该方法在PyTorch基础上实现0.88x-6.72x速度提升，相较于最先进的内核优化智能体最高达1.4x加速，并可将优化时间降低最多48%。

Conclusion: 该设计通过分层评估与资源分配策略，在提升内核质量的同时提高了搜索效率，展示了实用化GPU内核自动优化的可行路径。

Abstract: Automating GPU kernel optimization remains difficult in practice: generated variants can violate correctness constraints, runtime measurements are noisy, and search often stalls early. We present a practical optimization agent that combines LLM-guided mutation, adaptive resource allocation, policy-gated evaluation, and profiler-informed diagnosis. The system screens many candidates with low-cost evaluation and allocates higher-fidelity budget only to promising survivors to optimize and evolve GPU kernels. On important Triton kernel generation tasks, this design improves both kernel quality and search efficiency, reaching 0.88x-6.72x speedup over PyTorch and up to 1.4x speedup over the state-of-the-art kernel agent, with up to 48% lower optimization time.

</details>


### [107] [Axiomatic shared-medium coordination for stigmergic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02619)
*Fernando Paredes García*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与具体媒介无关的随机协同（stigmergic）协调比较框架，以"抽象使能-响应签名"（abstract enabled-response signature）作为比较对象，并给出了正/负两个方向的定理：粗商（coarse quotient）的响应充分性（response-adequate）与纤维（fiber）对齐之间的等价关系，以及失败情形下的规范最粗修复及其下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机协同协调在许多媒介（如元组空间、时间戳虚拟数据空间等）中有各自特化的形式化定义，缺乏统一的跨媒介比较机制，难以在不同媒介实现之间进行语义等价性或保真度的系统比较。

Method: 构建一个媒介无关的比较层，以抽象使能-响应签名为比较对象。在元数据精化（metadata refinement）方面，证明粗商响应充分当且仅当每个商纤维内部响应对齐（response-aligned）；当该条件不成立时，构造规范最粗的响应充分修复（coarsest response-adequate repair），并证明任何修复必须保留至少对应纤维级响应类数量。在动态语义方面，研究商兼容的匹配写入（quotient-compatible matched writes）保持单步行为、新鲜度条件动作（freshness-conditional actions）产生单步不可提升（non-liftability）障碍，并将匹配单步结果推广到有限串行迹（finite serial traces）。

Result: 给出了响应充分性与纤维对齐的充要条件定理、失败时的最粗修复唯一性及下界结果、单步保持与不可提升障碍定理、以及到有限串行迹的提升定理。并在元组空间媒介和时间戳虚拟随机协同数据空间两个具体媒介上进行了实例化验证。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个媒介无关的随机协同协调比较框架，附带一个明确的正/负定理循环（positive/negative theorem cycle）；更丰富的路径级（pathwise）和并发（concurrent）语义超出当前研究范围，留待未来工作。

Abstract: Stigmergic coordination has many medium-specific formalizations. This paper formulates a medium-agnostic comparison layer whose comparison object is the abstract enabled-response signature. For metadata refinements, we prove that a coarse quotient is response-adequate exactly when each quotient fiber is response-aligned. When that fails, there is a canonical coarsest response-adequate repair, and every response-adequate repair must retain at least the corresponding fiberwise number of response classes. On the dynamic side, quotient-compatible matched writes preserve one-step behavior, freshness-conditional actions yield a one-step non-liftability obstruction, and the matched one-step result lifts to finite serial traces. These results are instantiated for a tuple-space medium and a timestamped virtual-stigmergic dataspace. The result is a medium-agnostic comparison framework with one explicit positive/negative theorem cycle; richer pathwise and concurrent semantics remain outside scope.

</details>


### [108] [Configurable and Hierarchical Allreduce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02884)
*Valentino Guerrini,Ke Fan,Sidharth Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CHIARA，一种可配置的分层Allreduce算法，通过逻辑批次-车道拓扑编码硬件层次，并采用分阶段调度与旋转根车道原语，在不同超算上相比厂商MPI_Allreduce获得最高13.48x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI_Allreduce是大规模科学计算和分布式机器学习中最关键的性能集体通信原语之一，但在小到中等消息规模下仍面临严峻挑战：延迟开销、同步深度以及域内/域间通信的强硬件层次差异加剧了单次调用的成本。

Method: CHIARA采用可配置的分层Allreduce设计：1) 通过逻辑批次-车道（batch-lane）拓扑编码硬件层次；2) 执行分段调度，任意时刻仅激活约简向量中的有界部分；3) 通过旋转根车道原语将跨批次通信分布到多个rank，避免集中式leader；4) 支持半组合的Rabenseifner风格Allreduce，在Reduce-Scatter/Allgather边界保留车道对齐的中间布局，消除冗余的域内重组。

Result: 在Polaris、Aurora和Fugaku上评估，相比厂商MPI_Allreduce分别获得最高1.94x、13.43x和13.48x加速；在并行k-means应用中实现最高2.2x端到端加速。

Conclusion: CHIARA通过编码硬件层次和分阶段调度，有效解决了小到中等消息规模下Allreduce的延迟与同步瓶颈，显示了显著优于厂商实现和实际应用的性能提升。

Abstract: MPI_Allreduce is among the most performance-critical collectives in large-scale scientific computing and distributed machine learning, yet the small- and medium-message regime remains challenging: latency, synchronization depth, and strong hardware hierarchy between intra- and inter-domain communication all compound per-invocation cost. We present CHIARA, a configurable hierarchical Allreduce that encodes hardware hierarchy through a logical batch-lane topology and executes a staged schedule in which only a bounded portion of the reduction vector is active at a time. Inter-batch communication is distributed across multiple ranks via a rotating-root lane primitive, avoiding centralized leaders. Tool further enables a semi-composed Rabenseifner-style Allreduce by preserving a lane-aligned intermediate layout across the Reduce-Scatter/Allgather boundary, eliminating redundant intra-domain reorganization. We evaluate Tool on Polaris, Aurora, and Fugaku, achieving speedups of up to 1.94x, 13.43x, and 13.48x over vendor MPI_Allreduce, and up to 2.2x end-to-end speedup in a parallel k-means application.

</details>


### [109] [Distributed Algorithms for Near-Equitable Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02910)
*Amit Nir,David Peleg*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式网络中的近均衡图着色问题。对于一个最大度为Δ、直径为D的n顶点图，均衡(Δ+1)-着色要求每种颜色的使用频率尽量接近σ=n/(Δ+1)。Hajnal-Szemerédi定理保证此类着色总是存在，且已知有O(n²Δ)时间的顺序算法。本文在分布式模型（CONGEST和Congested Clique）下，探索调色板大小逼近Δ+1与着色频率接近σ之间的权衡，并提出了一系列快速随机分布式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式网络中计算均衡着色是一个核心问题，但传统的均衡(Δ+1)-着色在分布式环境下计算成本高。作者希望探索在保持调色板大小接近Δ+1的同时，能否用更少的分布式轮次计算出频率接近均匀的着色，并研究这两个相互冲突的目标之间的权衡关系。

Method: 作者提出了多套随机的分布式算法，这些算法代表了调色板大小与频率均匀性权衡曲线上的不同点。算法在顺序（sequential）、CONGEST和Congested Clique模型中分别进行了设计，分析了各自的属性（如频率偏差、调色板大小偏差）和时间复杂度。

Result: 本文揭示了调色板大小（接近Δ+1）与频率均匀性（接近σ）之间存在明显的权衡关系，并给出了在不同权衡点上具有不同时间复杂度的分布式算法集合，覆盖了多个分布式计算模型。

Conclusion: 在分布式设置下，均衡着色的调色板大小与颜色频率的均匀性之间存在固有的权衡，本文提供了一系列随机分布式算法来探索这一权衡空间，展示了在不同分布式模型下如何通过调整参数在这两个目标之间进行取舍。

Abstract: For an $n$-vertex graph of maximum degree $Δ$ and diameter $D$, an equitable $(Δ+1)$-coloring is a vertex coloring where the frequency of each color (namely, the number of vertices it colors) are all equal to $σ=n/(Δ+1)$ (up to rounding). The Hajnal-Szemerédi Theorem guarantees the existence of such a coloring for every graph, and an $O(n^2Δ)$ time sequential algorithm is known for computing such a coloring. Here, we study near-equitable graph coloring in distributed networks. The main question of interest is how close one can remain to the desired palette size of $Δ+1$ while computing, in few distributed rounds, a coloring whose frequencies are close to $σ$. It appears that these two conflicting parameters exhibit a tradeoff, which we attempt to explore. We present a suite of fast randomized distributed algorithms representing varying points on this tradeoff, analyze their properties, and study their time complexity in the sequential, CONGEST and Congested Clique (CC) models.

</details>


### [110] [Diameter-Free Distributed Frequency Control for Graph Coloring in the CONGEST Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02920)
*Amit Nir,David Peleg*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出两个随机着色算法，在同步CONGEST模型中控制颜色频率，无需付出依赖网络直径的协调代价。两个算法均以高概率输出满足频率约束的合法着色，运行时间为O((λ+1)logn)轮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式着色问题中，传统的颜色频率控制方法通常需要依赖网络直径进行全局协调，代价高昂。本文旨在设计不依赖直径的颜色频率控制算法，同时保证合法着色和频率偏差的统计保证。

Method: 第一个算法使用χ=⌈(2+δ)Δ⌉种颜色，以概率至少1-n^(-λ)输出合法着色，将每种颜色频率与n/χ的偏差控制在O_δ(√((λ+1)(n/χ)lgn)+(λ+1)lgn)范围内；在显式负载条件下可得到双侧相对均衡。第二个算法适用于任意χ>Δ，提供由调色板余量χ-Δ控制的单侧频率上限；特别地，使用Δ+⌈(Δ+1)/⌈ln n⌉⌉种颜色时，将每种颜色类大小限制在O((λ+1)(σlg²n+lgn))，其中σ=n/(Δ+1)。

Result: 两个算法均运行在O((λ+1)lgn)轮内，且不依赖网络直径；第一个算法的常数因子依赖于δ。算法为随机化算法，失败概率受参数λ控制（指数级小失败概率）。

Conclusion: 本文表明在同步CONGEST模型中，可以在不付出直径依赖协调代价的前提下，通过随机化方法有效控制颜色频率的偏差，同时保持高效的时间复杂度，为分布式均衡着色问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents two randomized proper-coloring algorithms that control color frequencies in the synchronous CONGEST model without paying a diameter-dependent coordination cost. Let $λ\geq 1$ denote the desired failure exponent. For every fixed $δ> 0$, the first algorithm uses $χ= \lceil (2+δ)Δ\rceil$ colors and, with probability at least $1 - n^{-λ}$, outputs a proper coloring that bounds the deviation of every color frequency from $n/χ$ by $O_δ(\sqrt{(λ+1)(n/χ)\lg n} + (λ+1)\lg n)$. Under an explicit load condition, this additive guarantee yields two-sided relative balance. The second algorithm works with every $χ> Δ$ and gives a one-sided frequency cap controlled by the palette slack $χ- Δ$. In particular, it uses $Δ+ \lceil (Δ+1)/\lceil \ln n \rceil \rceil$ colors and caps every used color class by $O((λ+1)(σ\lg^2 n + \lg n))$, where $σ= n/(Δ+1)$. Both algorithms run in $O((λ+1)\lg n)$ rounds, with no dependence on the network diameter; for the first algorithm, the multiplicative constant in the time bound depends on $δ$.

</details>


### [111] [Pruning-Aware Multi-Cluster Co-Inference for Large AI Models in AI-RANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03026)
*Xiaowen Cao,Zhonghao Lyu,Shicheng Chu,Zezhong Zhang,Dingzhu Wen,Guangxu Zhu,Kaibin Huang,Shuguang Cui,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种多集群大规模AI模型协同推理框架，边缘服务器协调多个用户集群协同执行推理任务，通过联合优化模型剪枝比例、任务调度、带宽分配和传输功率，在满足时延、能耗和服务器容量约束下最小化推理失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI模型的规模扩大和计算需求增长，在资源受限的分布式环境中进行高效推理面临重大挑战，亟需一种能够协调多集群资源、在有限条件下实现高精度推理的协同框架。

Method: 提出多集群LAIM协同推理框架：集群内设备从多视角采集数据并用轻量级端侧LAIM提取局部特征，上传至配备多GPU的边缘服务器进行聚合融合。利用率失真理论和部分信息分解构建理论框架刻画模型剪枝与推理性能之间的权衡，在此基础上构建联合优化问题，联合决策模型剪枝比例、任务调度策略、带宽分配和传输功率，以最小化推理失真。

Result: 大量仿真结果表明，所提框架在推理精度和资源效率方面显著优于现有基准方案。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在多集群边缘智能网络中实现更高的推理准确性和资源效率，为解决大规模AI模型在分布式环境下的高效推理问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The increasing scale and computational demands of large artificial intelligence models (LAIMs) present significant challenges for efficient inference in resource-constrained distributed environments. In this paper, we propose a multi-cluster LAIM co-inference framework, where an edge server equipped with multiple graphics processing units (GPUs) coordinates multiple user clusters to execute inference tasks collaboratively. Within each cluster, devices capture data from diverse perspectives and employ lightweight on-device LAIMs to extract local features. These features are then transmitted to the edge server, where they are aggregated and fused to generate a more accurate inference outcome. To reveal the fundamental trade-off between model pruning and collaborative inference performance, we develop a theoretical framework that characterizes the impact of pruning ratios and device contributions using rate-distortion theory and partial information decomposition. Based on this analysis, we formulate a joint optimization problem that determines the model pruning ratio, the task scheduling strategy, the bandwidth allocation, and the transmission power, with the goal of minimizing the inference distortion while satisfying the constraints of latency, energy consumption, and server capacity. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly outperforms existing benchmark schemes, achieving superior inference accuracy and resource efficiency in multi-cluster edge intelligence networks.

</details>


### [112] [Trust-Aware Topology Learning for Dynamic Decentralized Federated Learning under Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03156)
*Shubham Vaishnav,Murtaza Rangwala,Ali Beikmohammadi,Sindri Magnússon,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DMTT是构建在MURMURA之上的去中心化个性化联邦学习协议，通过证据深度学习与可信拓扑筛选机制（融合链接可靠性估计、签名拓扑声明、证人证实和Beta分布源信任模型），在动态移动去中心化联邦学习场景中同时抵御模型更新投毒和拓扑操纵攻击。理论证明了筛选混合矩阵可将拜占庭影响限制在有界残差δ_max内，在完美筛选下趋于零；实验证明在UCI HAR和PAMAP2上，面对10-80%的敌方比例，DMTT保持诚实节点准确率在0.862和0.829以上，是唯一在所有比例下超越局部基线的方法，静态/动态FedAvg在任意比例下崩溃至随机猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态移动去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中，攻击者不仅能投毒模型更新，还能操纵设备用于选择协作方的拓扑信息。现有的鲁棒聚合器（Krum、BALANCE、UBAR）在面对动态图和拓扑操纵攻击时无法稳定超越局部训练基线，而静态/动态FedAvg在敌对环境任意比例下即崩溃。因此需要一种同时防御模型级和拓扑级双重投毒攻击的去中心化个性化联邦学习协议。

Method: DMTT基于MURMURA协议扩展，核心创新包括：(1) 每个设备维护一个基于链接可靠性估计、签名拓扑声明和证人证实的置信加权局部拓扑视图；(2) 使用Beta分布源信任模型评估信息来源的可信度；(3) 通过综合评分（融合模型兼容性、拓扑信任和链接可靠性）筛选可信协作方集合；(4) 仅对通过信任筛选的协作方进行聚合，使用证据深度学习降权分布不匹配的节点；(5) 理论上证明筛选混合矩阵可将拜占庭影响限制在有界残差δ_max内；(6) 实现为无协调器分布式系统，每个客户端为独立ZeroMQ进程，由共享墙钟周期同步。

Result: 在UCI HAR和PAMAP2数据集（各分割为100个移动客户端，Dirichlet异质性）上，DMTT在10-80%所有测试敌方比例下保持诚实节点准确率高于0.862（UCI HAR）和0.829（PAMAP2），低敌方比例时接近无攻击准确率，极端比例时优雅退化为局部训练性能。静态和动态FedAvg在所有敌方比例下崩溃到随机猜测；Krum、BALANCE、UBAR等鲁棒聚合器无法一致超越局部基线。DMTT是唯一在所有比例和两个数据集上都超越该基线的方案，且存活的拜占庭聚合权重经验上为零，与δ_max=0的理论一致。

Conclusion: DMTT通过将拓扑信任机制与模型兼容性筛选相结合，在动态移动DFL中实现了对模型级和拓扑级双重投毒攻击的鲁棒防御。理论界（δ_max）与实际经验（存活拜占庭权重为零）的一致性验证了方法的有效性，且系统在真实节点上通过无协调器的ZeroMQ后端端到端运行，证明了其实用性。

Abstract: In dynamic mobile decentralized federated learning (DFL), adversaries can poison both model updates and the topology information devices use to choose collaborators. We present DMTT (Dynamic MURMURA with Trusted Topology), a decentralized personalized FL protocol built on MURMURA, which uses evidential deep learning to down-weight distribution-mismatched peers, extended here to time-varying graphs under topology-manipulation attacks. Each device maintains a confidence-weighted local topology view from link-reliability estimates, signed topology claims, witness corroboration, and a Beta-distributed source-trust model, then aggregates only over a trust-screened collaborator set using a composite score fusing model compatibility, topology trust, and link reliability. We prove the screened mixing matrices confine Byzantine influence to a bounded residual $δ_{max}$ that vanishes under perfect screening, and implement DMTT as a coordinator-free distributed system with each client running as an independent ZeroMQ process synchronized by a shared wall-clock epoch. On UCI HAR and PAMAP2, each partitioned across 100 mobile clients with Dirichlet heterogeneity, DMTT sustains honest-node accuracy above 0.862 (UCI HAR) and 0.829 (PAMAP2) across all tested adversary fractions (10 to 80%), nearly matching no-attack accuracy at low fractions and degrading gracefully toward local-only performance at extremes; static and dynamic FedAvg collapse to chance at every fraction, and robust aggregators (Krum, BALANCE, UBAR) fail to consistently beat a local-only baseline, while DMTT is the only method that clears this bar across both datasets at all fractions, with surviving Byzantine aggregation weight empirically zero throughout, consistent with $δ_{max}$=0. The protocol runs end-to-end on real nodes via a coordinator-free ZeroMQ backend on the Melbourne Research Cloud.

</details>


### [113] [LPV Control for Dynamic Power Capping in High-Performance Computing under Mixed Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03367)
*Mohamed Abdeldjalil Maziz,Kouds Halitim,Bogdan Robu,Sophie Cerf*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了HPC系统中动态功耗调节策略，对比了增益调度PI控制器和基于H∞控制的仿射LPV控制器，两者均由工作负载指示器调度。实验表明两种控制器均能遵守功率限制，但LPV控制器在跟踪误差、控制方差和相变瞬态平滑度方面优于增益调度PI控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC系统中平衡能耗与性能仍是关键挑战。静态功耗上限机制（如Intel RAPL）缺乏对动态变化工作负载的适应性，需要探索更灵活的动态功耗调节策略。

Method: 研究了两种反馈策略：一种是增益调度比例积分（PI）控制器，另一种是通过H∞控制综合的仿射线性参数变化（LPV）控制器，两者均由在存储阶段与计算阶段之间切换的工作负载指示器进行调度。在实际功耗上限约束下评估了跟踪性能、相位切换和鲁棒性。

Result: 两种控制器均能遵守功耗限制，但LPV设计在跟踪误差、控制方差和相位变化期间的瞬态平滑度方面优于增益调度PI控制器。

Conclusion: LPV控制器在混合工作负载场景下的动态功耗调节中展现出更优的综合性能，为HPC系统的自适应功耗管理提供了更有效的方案。

Abstract: Balancing energy consumption and performance remains a critical challenge in High Performance Computing (HPC) systems. While static power capping mechanisms such as Intel's Running Average Power Limit (RAPL) offer basic control capabilities, they lack the flexibility to adapt to dynamically varying workloads. This work studies dynamic power regulation for mixed workload scenarios. We investigate two feedback strategies: a gain scheduled proportional-integral (PI) controller and a polytopic linear parameter-varying (LPV) controller synthesized via H$\infty$ control, both scheduled by a workload indicator that changes between memory and compute phase. We evaluate tracking performance, phase switching, and robustness under practical power cap constraints. While both controllers respect power limits, the LPV design achieves lower tracking error, lower control variance, and smoother transients during phase changes than gain scheduled PI.

</details>


### [114] [FedRings: A Scalable and Topology-Aware Federated Learning Framework for LEO Satellite Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03436)
*Ziwu Liu,Inês Pinto Gouveia,Rehana Yasmin,Paulo Esteves-Verissimo,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出FedRings，一种面向LEO卫星网络的去中心化联邦学习框架，通过环形通信结构、时空路由策略、自适应稀疏增量聚合和历史补偿机制，解决动态拓扑下集中式训练效率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络中链路频繁变化、接触时间短、拓扑高度动态，导致集中式或同步式联邦学习训练效率低下且难以扩展。

Method: 1) 将卫星组织为环形通信结构（去中心化）；2) 采用结合链路感知通信调度的时空路由策略，使模型交换与实际可见窗口和时变连接模式对齐；3) 沿环传播模型更新，使用自适应稀疏增量聚合，通过逐步组合和压缩更新降低通信开销；4) 引入历史补偿机制以应对通信中断、维持训练连续性。

Result: 实验表明FedRings在真实场景下一致优于现有方法，同时降低通信成本，实现动态LEO网络中稳定高效的联邦学习。

Conclusion: 结合拓扑感知路由、通信调度和高效聚合，FedRings能够在不稳定且高度动态的LEO卫星网络环境中实现稳定、低通信成本的去中心化联邦学习。

Abstract: Federated learning over low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks is limited by frequent link changes, short contact times, and a highly dynamic topology, making centralized or synchronized training inefficient and hard to scale. To address this, we propose FedRings, a decentralized framework that organizes satellites into ring-based communication structures. It uses a spatio-temporal routing strategy with link-aware communication scheduling to align model exchange with actual visibility windows and time-varying connectivity patterns in LEO. Model updates are propagated along the ring using adaptive sparse incremental aggregation, which reduces communication overhead by progressively combining and compressing updates. To handle communication interruptions, a historical compensation mechanism maintains training continuity. By combining topology-aware routing, communication scheduling, and efficient aggregation, FedRings enables stable and efficient learning in dynamic LEO networks while reducing communication cost, and experiments show it consistently outperforms existing methods in realistic settings.

</details>


### [115] [TAOT: Topology-Aware Optimal Transport for Dynamic Expert Replica Placement in MoE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03676)
*Lingyun Zhang,Henghua Zhang,Shilei Gu,Kai Mo,Shuai Han,Shiyong Li,Yanpeng Wang,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TAOT是一种拓扑感知的最优传输方法，用于动态专家副本放置，通过将热点专家和空闲容量的负载均衡建模为带通信成本矩阵的熵正则化最优传输问题，并用Sinkhorn-Knopp迭代求解，从而在MoE训练中同时优化负载均衡和跨节点通信成本，实现1.43倍端到端加速和最高74%的通信成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态副本方法只优化负载均衡，忽略了跨节点拓扑中移动专家权重的通信成本，导致通信开销可能抵消均衡带来的收益，使训练成本反而增加。

Method: 将热点rank上的过载和轻载rank上的空闲容量建模为带通信成本矩阵的平衡熵正则化最优传输问题；用Sinkhorn-Knopp迭代求解得到rank级流提示；将整数副本匹配与token分配组合为可执行调度；在系统层面将访客权重传输与主专家计算重叠以隐藏通信开销。

Result: TAOT实现1.43倍端到端MoE训练加速；均衡质量达到或优于现有最先进方法；在所有配置中取得最低的加权专家通信成本，最高降低74%。

Conclusion: 通过拓扑感知的最优传输框架，将负载均衡与通信成本联合优化，证明了在动态专家副本放置中考虑通信代价可以显著提升MoE训练效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become a key architecture for scaling large language models (LLMs), yet its dynamic routing causes severe load imbalance in expert-parallel training. Existing dynamic-replica methods copy hot experts onto idle ranks to share computation, but they optimize load balance alone and ignore the cost of moving expert weights across a multi-node topology, so the resulting cross-node communication can outweigh the balancing gain and inflate training cost. We present TAOT, a topology-aware optimal transport method for dynamic expert-replica placement. TAOT models the overload on hot ranks and the spare capacity on lightly loaded ranks as a balanced entropy-regularized optimal transport problem with a communication-cost matrix, solves it with Sinkhorn-Knopp iterations to produce rank-level flow hints, and combines integer replica matching with token assignment into an executable schedule. At the system level, it overlaps guest-weight transfer with home-expert computation to hide the communication overhead. Experiments show TAOT achieves a 1.43x end-to-end MoE training speedup, reaches balance quality competitive with or better than existing state-of-the-art methods, and attains the lowest weighted expert-communication cost across all configurations, with up to a 74% reduction.

</details>


### [116] [Accelerating Dynamic Graph Clustering on GPU Architectures with cuGraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03695)
*Nelson Aloysio Reis de Almeida Passos,Emanuele Carlini,Salvatore Trani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究针对时序网络中的社区检测问题，基于NVIDIA RAPIDS生态构建GPU加速框架，通过Leiden贪心优化和对称Bethe-Hessian算子特征分解两种方法处理快照式动态图，实现多GPU支持，相比CPU参考在同等计算预算下可获得约三个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序网络中的社区检测对于流行病传播、金融系统、网络安全、轨迹与移动分析等应用领域至关重要，但现有方法大多针对静态图设计，缺乏对动态图的GPU加速支持，计算效率成为瓶颈。

Method: 基于NVIDIA RAPIDS生态构建GPU加速框架，采用两种核心算法：(1) Leiden贪心优化算法，通过Dask进行基于工作负载分布的多GPU支持；(2) 对称Bethe-Hessian算子的特征分解。同时引入多切片模块化（multislice modularity）后端，并保持与现有图分析流水线的兼容性，提供Python绑定。

Result: 多切片模块化后端在相同计算预算下相比CPU参考实现可获得约三个数量级（约1000倍）的加速，加速效果取决于图密度和快照数量；在真实世界和合成数据集上验证了适用性，支持时序上结构网络属性的探索性分析。

Conclusion: 该GPU加速框架有效解决了时序网络社区检测的计算瓶颈，以免费开源软件形式发布，并通过NetworkX-Temporal库提供Python绑定，可实现现有代码库的零代码加速，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: This work addresses community detection in temporal networks through GPU-accelerated extensions of spectral clustering and modularity-based algorithms originally designed for static graphs. Built on the NVIDIA RAPIDS ecosystem, the framework enables the characterization and tracking of communities in snapshot-based dynamic graphs, either by Leiden greedy optimization with multi-GPU support via Dask-based workload distribution, or eigendecomposition of a symmetric Bethe-Hessian operator. Our multislice modularity backend achieves up to roughly three orders of magnitude speedup over the CPU reference under an equal-work budget, depending on graph density and snapshot count, while preserving compatibility with existing graph analytics pipelines. We demonstrate its applicability on real-world and synthetic datasets, facilitating exploratory analysis of structural network properties over time. Such capabilities are relevant across several application domains, such as epidemic spreading, financial systems, cybersecurity, and trajectory and mobility analysis. We release our implementation as free and open-source software, including Python bindings through the NetworkX-Temporal library for ease of use and zero-code acceleration with existing codebases.

</details>


### [117] [When Does Disaggregation Pay? Simulating Prefill--Decode--Attention--FFN Specialization for Agentic LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03741)
*Przemyslaw Forys,Haoran Wu,Can Xiao,Jiayi Nie,Tony Liu,Rika Antonova,Timothy Jones,Robert Mullins,Wayne Luk,Aaron Zhao,George A. Constantinides*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HeteroPanacea仿真框架，用于探索异构解耦（disaggregated）推理系统中各组件的最优硬件配置，支持分阶段量化、自动并行调度和NPU架构异质性三維度的系统级仿真。实验表明Prefill-Decode解耦相比传统GPU服务吞吐量最高提升75%，而四路PDAF解耦在跨模型吞吐提升上最为一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic推理已成为LLM推理的主流范式，其多轮交互和工具调用特性使得prefill与decode阶段在计算和内存带宽需求上差异显著，传统同构GPU系统难以高效支撑。行业正转向异构解耦系统（如Vera-Rubin平台），但异构系统中各组件的最优硬件配置尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出HeteroPanacea仿真框架，在三个维度上进行系统级建模：1）解耦分阶段的量化精度配置；2）自动化的设备内与设备间并行化调度；3）PDAF（prefill-decode-attention-FFN）四阶段NPU架构异质性。通过这三个维度的组合提供跨栈仿真能力，并辅以消融实验研究模型架构与解耦收益之间的关系。

Result: 验证了Prefill-Decode解耦相比传统GPU统一服务的优势，仿真显示吞吐量最高可提升75%；四路PDAF解耦在不同模型上是最稳定的吞吐提升方案；消融实验揭示了模型架构与解耦收益间的关联规律。

Conclusion: HeteroPanacea为未来异构Agentic推理服务系统提供了跨栈仿真框架，确认了解耦服务在异构硬件下的显著收益，并指出了PDAF四路解耦作为最一致的吞吐提升路径，为异构推理系统设计和硬件选型提供指导。

Abstract: Agentic inference now dominates the LLM inference landscape, requiring LLMs to actively engage in multi-turn interactions with tool-calling capabilities. This introduces a more complex workload for the underlying inference system: serving stages such as prefill and decode exhibit substantially different behaviors and demand distinct compute and memory-bandwidth capabilities. As a result, a single homogeneous GPU system now struggles to support agentic inference, motivating an industry shift toward heterogeneous systems with disaggregated serving capabilities, such as the emerging Vera-Rubin platform with GPUs and Groq LPUs. However, the question of what the optimal hardware should look like for each component in a heterogeneous system remains underexplored. To this end, we propose a novel simulation framework for disaggregated serving, termed \textbf{HeteroPanacea}, that enables system-level simulation across three dimensions: 1) disaggregated quantization, 2) automated intra- and inter-device parallelization scheduling, and 3) PDAF (prefill-decode-attention-FFN) NPU architectural heterogeneity. By combining these three axes, we provide a cross-stack simulation framework for future heterogeneous agentic serving systems. We confirm the benefit of Prefill Decode disaggregation, simulating increased serving throughput by up to 75\% compared to traditional serving with current GPUs and demonstrate 4 way Prefill Decode Attention FFN disaggregation is the most consistent for increasing throughput across different models, assuming custom NPUs. We also investigate the relationship between model architecture and gain from disaggregation by running a set of ablation studies.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [ISEE: Interactive Semantic Enrichment for Database Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02604)
*Yuan Tian,Yiru Chen,Rakesh R. Menon,Zifan Liu,Ting Cai,Fei Wu,Anudeep Chimakurthi,Prashanthi Ramamurthy,Sridevi Aishwariya Ganesan,Kun Qian,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为ISEE（Interactive SEmantic Enrichment）的交互式语义增强系统，用于解决LLM智能体在数据任务中因字段描述不完整或模糊而导致的性能瓶颈。该系统通过评分机制评估字段描述质量，收集用户领域知识，并与用户协作丰富数据语义，从而提升下游任务（如实体链接）的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在数据任务上的表现高度依赖数据语义的清晰度和完整性。然而实际中许多字段描述模糊或不完整，因为这些关键上下文（如自定义字段的含义）源于用户的领域知识，很少被公开记录。这种语义鸿沟限制了智能体在下游任务（如实体链接）中的表现。

Method: ISEE系统包含三个核心模块：(1) 基于评分系统衡量数据字段描述的质量；(2) 收集并整合用户的领域知识；(3) 与用户协作交互式地丰富数据语义。评估方法包括用户研究、自动化用户模拟、定量评估和案例研究。

Result: 实验表明，ISEE显著降低了用户的认知负荷，提升了字段描述的质量，并增强了下游任务（如实体链接）的性能。

Conclusion: 交互式语义增强是弥合数据字段描述与领域知识鸿沟的有效途径，ISEE通过人机协作实现了数据语义的完善，从而释放LLM智能体在数据任务中的潜力。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed for data-related tasks, including data sense-making, exploration, and retrieval. However, their performance heavily depends on the clarity and completeness of data semantics. In practice, many field descriptions remain ambiguous or incomplete, as much of the essential context (e.g., the meaning of a customized field) originates from users' domain knowledge and is rarely documented publicly. This gap restricts the agents' task performance in downstream tasks, such as entity-linking. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel and comprehensive Interactive SEmantic Enrichment system (ISEE). Given a data field description, ISEE measures its quality through a scoring system, gathers domain knowledge, and collaboratively enriches the semantics with users. Through a user study, automated user simulation, quantitative evaluation, and case study, we demonstrate that ISEE significantly reduces cognitive load, improves description quality, and enhances downstream task performance.

</details>


### [119] [Self-Organising Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02606)
*Marcello Barylli,Gabriel Béna,Alexander Mordvintsev,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自组织数字电路（Self-Organising Digital Circuits），将功能逻辑生成与维护建模为图上的元学习问题，采用拓扑掩码Transformer来配置电路布尔门的查找表（LUT），从而模拟生物系统的自适应可塑性，实现从零自组装功能电路并在硬件故障下快速重新路由逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典计算中的容错传统上依赖静态策略（如硬件冗余和纠错码），而生物系统展现出适应性可塑性，能够通过围绕损伤的动态重组来维持功能。作者希望借鉴这一生物学原理，克服静态容错方法缺乏灵活性的局限。

Method: 提出自组织数字电路架构，将功能性逻辑生成和维护形式化为图上的元学习问题。采用拓扑掩码的Transformer来配置电路中布尔门的查找表（LUT），扩展神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式——在退化（退化）的布尔搜索空间中导航以满足计算任务，而非再生固定的目标状态。

Result: 该策略能够从零开始自组装功能电路；在永久性未见硬件故障下可快速重新路由逻辑；对于软错误，在远超训练条件的损伤规模下实现了接近完美的恢复（>99.99%准确率）；并观察到跨电路规模的泛化能力——在比训练时更宽的图上精度反而有所提升。

Conclusion: 这项工作将生物自组织原理与数字硬件的实际领域联系起来，为容错数字电路设计提供了一种受神经形态启发的动态、自适应新范式。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in classical computing has traditionally relied on static strategies like hardware redundancy and error-correcting codes. Biological systems, in contrast, exhibit adaptive plasticity, maintaining function through dynamic re-organisation around damage. Inspired by this principle, we introduce Self-Organising Digital Circuits, framing functional logic generation and maintenance as a meta-learning problem on graphs. Our architecture employs a topology-masked Transformer that configures the Lookup Tables (LUT) of a circuit's Boolean gates. Extending the pattern-generation paradigm of Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), it navigates the degenerate Boolean search space to satisfy a computational task, rather than regenerating a fixed target state. We demonstrate that it can self-assemble functional circuits from scratch and rapidly re-route logic around permanent, previously unseen hardware faults. For soft errors, the policy achieves near-perfect recovery (>99.99\% accuracy) from damage sizes far exceeding training conditions. We further observe generalisation across circuit scales: accuracy improves on graphs substantially wider than those seen during training. This work bridges the principles of biological self-organisation with the practical domain of digital hardware.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Meta-Persona Anchoring结合Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS)的框架，用于缓解大型语言模型中的"Artificial Hivemind"效应（即模型在开放性问题上的语义收敛和同质化）。该方法通过两阶段生成过程显著降低响应间的余弦相似度（从约0.85降至约0.65），提高了AI输出的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型存在"Artificial Hivemind"效应，即使在高温度采样下，模型也会在开放问题上收敛到狭窄、同质化的共识，导致高跨响应相似度（约0.80-0.90）。这种语义塌缩限制了AI输出的多样性，与人类水平的类型学多样性存在显著差距。

Method: 采用两阶段生成过程：(1) Meta-Persona Anchoring：提示模型自我选择独特的个性化角色作为生成起点；(2) Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS)：双阶段采样筛——先用Top-p过滤保留语法有效性，再对幸存候选施加极端温度缩放（T≥4.0）以探索拓宽的概率分布。在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上对约20B参数的先进开源权重模型进行评估。

Result: 平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，大部分问题低于0.7阈值，显著减少了语义收敛，缩小了人工模式塌缩与人类水平类型多样性之间的差距。代码已作为开源框架发布。

Conclusion: Meta-Persona Anchoring结合FTS是一种有效缓解LLM语义塌缩、增强输出多样性的方法，为实现更多样化和创造性的AI部署提供了可行的开源解决方案。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [121] [PULSE: An Executable Contract Language for Spatiotemporal Knowledge Graph Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02630)
*Dongxu Yang,Ziyi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PULSE是一种受Object-Process-Methodology启发的知识图谱工程语言，通过单一类型化运行时将四种操作角色及其写效果本地化，并提供一个外部执行者裁决证据是否成为权威操作的核心契约。该研究通过核心演算的效应限制引理和六个安全性质、Lean 4内核校验、88项测试及大规模时序轨迹验证，证明了契约本地化、安全论证和轨迹一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱工程中，被接受的状态、观测、约束、过程和假设场景通常分散在多个工件中，其组合执行契约是外部的、未显式定义的。这导致安全性质和运行时行为难以统一论证。作者希望设计一种语言，将操作角色及其写效果集中到一个类型化运行时中，从而本地化契约并支持形式化安全证明。

Method: 提出PULSE语言，基于Object-Process-Methodology，其中mode表示操作角色而非模态/道义逻辑。实现的契约包含：证据不可覆盖、分支隔离、基于多主体的计时器、受保护的状态变更、基于声明排名的时空事件排序。GeoSPARQL、SOSA和SHACL作为生成视图。定义一个核心演算，给出效应限制引理和六个安全性质。使用Lean 4检查内核类比（位置、证据、时钟、监视器、原子性、分支来源保留），并通过88项测试、3,534项有界检查和32个Lean/Python运行时-内核案例验证实现。

Result: 第一作者实现的组合标准和独立Sismic状态图复现了测试的冷链轨迹。在37,440条生成的时间轨迹中，PULSE与独立工作流匹配，并能区分十个单字段突变体。在NOAA IBTrACS自1980年的完整子集上，PULSE与GEOS和事件扫描在1,476,290个过渡区对（包括4,800个采样和12,831个持续时间限定事件）上达成一致。项目特定的GeoSPARQL探针测量了接口覆盖率。

Conclusion: 结果表明PULSE在测试片段上支持契约本地化、安全论证和轨迹一致性。但作者明确声明，语言的优越性和可用性不在本项目评估范围内，即本研究证明的是正确性与一致性，而非PULSE作为一种更优或更实用的语言。

Abstract: Knowledge graph engineering often distributes accepted state, observations, constraints, processes, and hypothetical scenarios across artifacts whose combined execution contract remains external. We present PULSE, an Object-Process-Methodology-inspired language that localizes four operational roles and their write effects in one typed runtime. Here, modes denote operational roles rather than modal or deontic logic. The implemented contract fixes evidence non-overwrite, branch isolation, grounded multi-subject timers, guarded state change, and declaration-ranked event ordering over time and space; an external runner still decides whether evidence becomes an authoritative move. GeoSPARQL, SOSA, and SHACL remain generated views. A core calculus gives an effect-confinement lemma and six safety properties. Lean 4 checks kernel analogues for positions, evidence, clocks, monitors, atomicity, and branch source retention; 88 tests, 3,534 bounded checks, and 32 Lean/Python runtime-kernel cases bound the implementation claim to the checked cases. First-author implementations of a standards composition and a separate Sismic statechart reproduce the tested cold-chain trace. Across 37,440 generated temporal traces, PULSE matches a separate workflow and distinguishes ten single-field mutants. On the complete NOAA IBTrACS since1980 subset it agrees with GEOS and an event sweep on 1,476,290 transition-zone pairs, including 4,800 sampled and 12,831 duration-qualified events. Project-specific GeoSPARQL probes measure interface coverage. Overall, the results support contract localization, safety arguments, and trace parity for the tested fragment; language superiority and usability remain outside the evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [HyperAgent: Planning and Acting over Tool-Schema Hypergraphs for Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02650)
*Zian Zhai,Xingyu Tan,Gaowang Zou,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperAgent框架，通过构建有向工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph）对工具间关系进行模式级建模，引导智能体进行动态规划与执行，在AppWorld基准上显著提升任务完成性能，同时减少冗余API调用、LLM交互和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具使用智能体通常依赖LLM从文本描述中推断工具组合方式，这种方式在面对复杂任务时存在隐式推理的局限性和执行环境动态变化的挑战，导致探索效率低下和执行不可靠。需要一种模式级别的工具关系建模方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 在模式层面构建有向工具-模式超图，将工具表示为从输入模式节点到输出模式节点的超边；2) 提出HyperAgent框架，首先从超图中提取任务相关的工具上下文图，据此构建模式感知的任务DAG；3) 在执行阶段，通过赤字导向扩展动态构建状态条件化的工具支持图，识别未解决的需求并根据当前智能体状态检索支持性生产者工具。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的实验表明，HyperAgent相比现有智能体基线提升了任务完成性能，同时减少了冗余API调用、LLM交互次数和token消耗。

Conclusion: 模式级别的工具-超图建模能够有效指导LLM智能体的动态规划与执行，在提升任务成功率的同时显著降低计算资源和交互成本，为工具使用智能体的可靠性提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external tools to complete complex real-world tasks. However, reliable tool-use planning remains challenging due to the limitations of implicit reasoning and the evolving nature of real-world execution environments. Existing tool-use agents typically rely on LLMs to infer tool compositions from textual descriptions, which can lead to inefficient exploration and unreliable execution in complex tasks. To address these challenges, we model tool relations at the schema level and construct a directed Tool--Schema Hypergraph, in which tools are represented as hyperedges from their required input-schema nodes to their output-schema nodes. Furthermore, we propose HyperAgent, a Tool--Schema Hypergraph-guided framework for dynamic planning and execution. Given a task, HyperAgent first extracts a task-relevant tool context graph and uses it to guide the construction of a schema-aware Task DAG. During execution, HyperAgent dynamically realizes each subtask by constructing a state-conditioned tool support graph through deficit-oriented expansion, which identifies unresolved requirements and retrieves supporting producer tools according to the current agent state. Experiments on AppWorld demonstrate that HyperAgent improves task completion performance while reducing redundant API calls, LLM interactions, and token consumption compared with existing agent baselines.

</details>


### [123] [Explainable AI for the EU Right to Explanation: A Systematic Review of the Law-XAI Translation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02699)
*Benjamin Fresz,Elena Dubovitskaya,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对欧盟"解释权"（Right to Explanation）背景下可解释AI（XAI）的文献进行了系统性综述，聚焦GDPR第15(1)(h)条和AI法案第86条，从2643篇初始记录中筛选出57篇全文并最终确定19篇同时具备法律与技术视角的论文。研究发现该领域存在三个问题模式，提出了"受众/目的框架"（Addressee/Purpose Framework）、四阶段操作化蓝图和六个开放研究问题，指出解释权面临沦为形式义务而无技术实现路径的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当算法影响贷款、招聘或医疗等重大决策时，欧盟法律赋予受影响个体"解释权"。然而可解释AI在实践中能否真正满足这一权利仍然缺乏理解。本文旨在系统审视XAI是否能满足欧盟法律中的解释权要求，因为这对个体挑战自动化决策的能力有直接影响。

Method: 本文采用系统性文献综述方法。由于AI法案最终版本于2024年7月发布且第86条为后期新增条款，文献检索限定为2024年起发表的论文。通过故意宽泛的搜索从2643篇初始记录中筛选，最终评审57篇全文，其中仅19篇体现了法律与技术视角的实质性整合。研究基于对这些论文的交叉分析识别出共性问题模式。

Result: 研究发现三个突出问题模式：（1）大多数论文错误识别了GDPR的法律依据；（2）很少论文涉及欧盟法院Dun & Bradstreet判决（可能因发表时间限制）；（3）解释形式（由受众决定）与内容（由法律目的决定）之间的区分经常被混淆。此外，仅19/2643篇（约0.7%）论文实现了法律与技术的实质性跨学科整合，揭示当前监管框架的跨学科综合存在明显空白。

Conclusion: 作者将上述问题概念化为"受众/目的框架"（Addressee/Purpose Framework），并据此提出四阶段操作化蓝图和六个具体开放研究问题。结论指出，若缺乏进一步进展，欧盟的解释权可能仍停留在形式义务层面，缺乏技术上可实现的合规路径。

Abstract: When algorithms make or influence consequential decisions---about loan eligibility, hiring, or healthcare---EU law grants affected individuals a Right to Explanation. Yet whether (and how) Explainable AI (XAI) can satisfy this right in practice remains poorly understood, with direct implications for individuals' ability to contest automated decisions that affect their lives. This paper presents a systematic literature review of XAI in the context of the EU Right to Explanation, with particular focus on Art. 15(1)(h) GDPR, Art. 86 AI Act (AIA), and related instruments. We consider papers published from 2024 onwards, as the final version of the AIA was published in July 2024---with Art. 86 being added late. From 2643 initial records identified by a deliberately broad search, we review 57 full texts, of which only 19 papers demonstrate substantive integration of both legal and technical perspectives, showing gaps in the interdisciplinary synthesis of the current regulatory framework.
